# EDHA handoff changelog — 2026-09

The dated session deltas of 2026-09 (95 deltas, 2026-09-04 → 2026-09-07), newest first,
moved VERBATIM out of `EDHA_FOUNDRY_HANDOFF.md` by `scripts/handoff-split.js` (TODO_REPO_HYGIENE
item 19b, ruling PM-R1). Nothing below the marker line is edited, summarised, or re-ordered.

- **What is true today** lives in the reference, `EDHA_FOUNDRY_HANDOFF.md` (root of the repo).
  A delta is the record of *what changed and why*; when the two disagree the newer delta wins
  and the reference owes a fix.
- **A new delta goes at the TOP of the current month's file**, directly under the marker line
  (iron rule 5). Do not add one to the handoff itself.
- **Finding a delta's original commit:** `git log --follow` on this file cannot reach it — the
  deltas left a file that still exists, and follow only tracks renames. Use the pickaxe on the
  old path instead: `git log -S"<delta title>" -- EDHA_FOUNDRY_HANDOFF.md` (the first commit
  listed, oldest, is where the delta was written). The index is `README.md` beside this file.

## 2026-09-07 — Item 92: Volatile Strike drops its `whenDamageType: "impact"` gate (R-88 (a), REBUILD + ⟳ Sync Talents)

**Root cause.** Bench run 42 (2026-09-07, PR #274) measured both directions on the deployed pack:
a plain weapon hit that dealt impact posted the *"⚡ Volatile Strike — 1 Investiture …"* offer, and
the same PC's ordinary keen sidesword hit posted nothing. `data/authored/leyline-red.json`'s
Volatile Strike block (`edha-on-hit` rule, `type: "edha-triggered-effect"`) carried R-23 (a)'s
`whenDealer: "any"` (item 58) **and** a `whenDamageType: "impact"` gate that R-23 never touched and
the card never mentions — the card's prose is the bare *"When you hit with a melee attack, spend 1
Investiture …"*. For a Red PC with a keen weapon the rider almost never fired.

**Fix.** Ben answered R-88 with "a" (dashboard, 2026-09-07 17:11): drop the gate, the card is
canon. Removed the `whenDamageType: "impact"` key from the rule's `handler` block and fixed the
rule's own `description` string (*"On a melee (impact) hit …"* → *"On a melee hit …"*). Checked
`data/leyline.json`'s Volatile Strike prose — already reads *"When you hit with a melee attack …"*
with no damage-type clause, so nothing there needed to change. This is the ONE authored-data
change the ruling licenses; no other talent or key touched.

**Proof.** Pack parity: built `edha-leyline` from `origin/main`'s data and from this branch's data
into separate scratch module roots, dumped both packs' embedded item/effect docs (via
`scripts/edha-pack-io.js`'s `readPack`), and diffed with build timestamps normalized out. Both
builds produced identical counts (365 talents, 136 items, 20 folders). **Exactly ONE document
differed: Volatile Strike**, and the only differences were the `whenDamageType` key's removal and
the `description` string change — zero folder docs differed. `node scripts/gates.js` green.

**REBUILD + ⟳ Sync Talents** — the leyline pack must be rebuilt and Ben's Red PCs re-synced before
this is live. 🤖 for the bench: checklist row **92-1** (Red section) — on the rebuilt pack, an
ordinary keen melee hit by a Volatile Strike owner should post the offer (bench 42's negative case,
flipped). Does not touch `EDHA_RULINGS.md` — item 91 records the R-88 answer. Does not touch
`tests/on-hit-dealer.test.js`'s `VOLATILE` fixture — it carries `whenDamageType: "impact"` to
exercise `edhaOnHitIsItemSpecific` as a test fixture, not an assertion against the authored file.

## 2026-09-07 — Item 89: `Unbreakable Line` gets its missing `use` rule on both blocks (REBUILD + ⟳ Sync Adversaries)

**Root cause.** Bench run 43 re-confirmed against the deployed pack: `Crownox Ring / Unbreakable
Line` and `The Reckoning / Unbreakable Line` each carried exactly one rule,
`edha-apply-watch → edha-gm-cue {rangeFt: 5}`. There was no `use` rule at all, so clicking the item
posted an **empty chat card** (`content: ""`) — no test, no contest core, no roll. The card's own
clause, *"the lead may test White (DC = half the damage) via the contest core,"* had never existed.
A second drift rode the same read: `The Reckoning` had `kind: "trait"` and no `cost` field, so it
built `activation.type: "none"` (Passive — cannot even be clicked), while `Crownox Ring` had
`cost: "Special"` / `consume: "3 Focus"` and built `"utility"` — the two Activation lines
(`* (3 Focus)` vs `8`) disagreed for the same named ability.

**Fix.** Both blocks now carry `events[].use → edha-def-test {skill: "white", vs: "prompt-dc",
requireTarget: true}` — the same contest-core primitive (H1) every other shipped "test X vs DC"
adversary ability uses (`ENGINE_INDEX.md` "H1 `edha-def-test`"), copying the Callthief's
Counterpoint shape. `vs: "prompt-dc"` rather than a flat `dc`: H1's DC field is read as
`Number(h.dc)` — a static number, no formula — and "DC = half the damage that dropped the ally"
varies per use, so there is no static form to write. `prompt-dc` asks the GM for the DC when the
White roll resolves, exactly matching the ability's own already-shipped note that "adversary
Investiture has no auto-write" (table-adjudicated), and is the closest expressible form (no engine
change needed or made). `The Reckoning` also dropped `kind: "trait"` and gained
`cost: "Special"` / `consume: "3 Focus"` to match Crownox Ring — every existing def-test-bearing
adversary ability in the file ships as the default "action" kind with an explicit cost, never
`"trait"`/Passive, and both adversary copies should mirror the PC talent's `3 Investiture`
(`data/leyline.json`, Focus is the adversary-side resource). Once the `use` rule names a skill,
`foundry-build.js`'s `advItemDoc` auto-promotes `activation.type` to `"skill_test"` for both
blocks regardless of `kind`/`cost` (the same 07-26j builder fix that rescued six other adversary
abilities), so the two Activation lines now agree without any hand-edited description text.

Neither block gained a success-payload rule (`edha-test-success`/`edha-test-fail`) — matching
Grand Deception's precedent (a def-test `use` rule with no payload siblings is legal; the engine's
own result card appends *"(no payload rule on this talent — resolve at the table)"*), and matching
The Reckoning's existing text that the hold is GM-adjudicated, not auto-written.

**Deploy class: REBUILD + ⟳ Sync Adversaries.** This is authored data
(`data/adversaries.json`), not engine code — nothing in `module-src/scripts/register-skills.js`
changed. `node scripts/gates.js` green, including `lint-refs.js` pass 5 (adversary wiring) and
pass 14 (a gated test must be able to roll — the derived skill matches the rule's skill on both
blocks).

🤖 **`EDHA_FOUNDRY_TEST_CHECKLIST.md` §2 "Unbreakable Line ally-drops cue"** — (b) half re-queued
for the next bench run with a REBUILD note: re-drive after Ben rebuilds the adversaries pack and
syncs, confirm a real contest-core roll posts on `use` (not an empty card) on both blocks, and
confirm the two Activation glyphs now match.

## 2026-09-07 — Item 86: `edhaListPlaceNotes` now sees weapon-borne `placeNote` riders too — gates on `edhaRuleBearer` (ENGINE-ONLY, F5)

**The other adjacent harvest loop item 84 named but did not fix.** Item 84's caller audit (PR #276)
flagged `edhaListPlaceNotes` (`module-src/scripts/engine/07-edha-owner-list.js:230`) as the same bug
shape one loop over: it walks every item on a ledger owner, skips `!edhaIsTalent`, then reads
`h.placeNote` off each remaining item's event rules to build the ⚖ Violated/PLACE card's
sibling-advertised hint (Sealed Edict's "you may notarize it", Lawkeeper's GM-reveal line). A
weapon-borne `placeNote` rider would be dropped exactly the way Surecat's `edha-on-hit` cue was.
**The fix is the one predicate swap**: `if (!edhaRuleBearer(item)) continue;`.

**Proven by mutation.** Four pins went into `tests/engine-helpers.test.js` (basic collection, the
list-key filter, an error-fallsafe, and the item-86 regression). Reverting the predicate to
`edhaIsTalent` in the assembled engine fails exactly the weapon pin — `node tests/run.js` →
**1026 passed, 1 failed** (`✗ edhaListPlaceNotes: a weapon-borne placeNote reaches the harvest
(item 86)`); re-running `node scripts/engine-assemble.js` to restore the fix returns the suite to
**1027 passed, 0 failed**. **0 shipped weapon-borne `placeNote` rules today** (counted across all
five packs) — this pin is the whole proof, so there is no checklist row to add.

**The rest of the sweep** — every remaining `edhaIsTalent(` call in `module-src/scripts/engine/*.js`,
verdicted per ENGINE_INDEX's standing rule (`edhaRuleBearer` for a rule-harvest loop, `edhaIsTalent`
for talent-*use* automation):
- **Talent-use automation, correct as is** (the `preUseItem`/`useItem`/`damageRoll` hook family —
  each fires on the ONE item the player is using, and weapons are excluded on purpose): the 13 cue
  gates in `05-edha-watch.js`; the H1 pre-use veto and burst-intercept pair in `04-black-ritual.js`
  and `39-burst-execution-the-gm-socket-relay.js`; the ambush-belief and Suture Cradle `useItem`
  hooks in `07-edha-owner-list.js`; the stance-toggle hook in `15-blue-calculation.js`; the Key-grant
  hook in `16-heroic-paths.js`; the single-target picker in
  `34-single-target-gate-defeat-tracking.js`; the cleanse-arm and Life/Chaos/Sovereignty deity hooks
  in `41-life.js`, `42-chaos.js`, `44-sovereignty.js`.
- **Other surface, left alone**: `01-shared-core.js:740` (Pack Advantage's ally talent-NAME parity
  check, not a rule-type scan); the three `24-the-character-creation-wizard.js` calls (the wizard
  only ever grants/removes TALENTS — Keys, path groups — weapons are the player's own gear pick,
  never a wizard-automated grant); `33-triggered-effect-resolution.js:201` (re-finds the
  ALREADY-KNOWN origin item by NAME to chain `edha-heal-react`, not a scan for any rule-bearing
  item); `35-targeting-attunement-range-aoe-templates.js:103` (the manual range-preview macro
  resolves a name string — a talent-only concept, weapons have their own reach); the two
  `53-native-event-system.js` lookups (`edha-enter-stance` and `summonByTalent`), both explicitly
  documented in their own comments as talent-only by design (`system.modality` / "looks up the named
  TALENT"); and `edhaCovBuffTemplate` (`49-order.js:374`), which item 84 already called out — it
  scans ActiveEffects, not event rules, a different surface entirely.

**ENGINE-ONLY — F5 or relaunch, no pack rebuild and no ⟳ Sync.** No authored data changed.
`node scripts/gates.js`: 11/11 PASS.

## 2026-09-07 — item 90: five harness-only checklist rows retired, no sideless token can exist (DOCS-ONLY)

Retired **I10b2-1, I10b2-2, I10b2-3, I10b2-4** and **77-1's** unset-side clause in
`EDHA_FOUNDRY_TEST_CHECKLIST.md`, accepting bench run 43's recommendation (`docs/BENCH_NEXT_RUN.md`
§3, offered but not acted on at the time). Bench runs 38, 42, and 43 each independently re-derived
the same fact on Ben's build (Foundry core 13.351, cosmere-rpg 2.1.0): a token's `disposition`
cannot be `null`/`undefined`/`NaN` — Foundry coerces all three to **−1 (HOSTILE)** at both create
and update, and an actor with no token is never in any range sweep either, so a "sideless" probe
cannot be staged. The five rows described an observable that cannot occur live on this build; the
behaviour they protect is pinned at the pure-function level in `tests/disposition-failclosed.test.js`
(23 cases covering every one of the five rows' underlying sites), which stands as the record.
77-1 is a single bare-bullet row (no `- [ ]` checkbox) whose aura-halves already PASSED at bench
run 42 — the unset-side clause was its only open part, so the whole row retires.
`docs/BENCH_NEXT_RUN.md` §3's recommendation paragraph marked done by item 90. Dashboard rebuilt:
Bench open 🤖 count dropped from 9 to 5 (four checkbox rows retired; 77-1 was never parsed as a
bench row — `build-dashboard.js`'s bench parser only turns `- [ ]` lines into items — so its
retirement is a documentation fix with no count effect). No engine, data, or ruling touched;
`EDHA_RULINGS.md` and `docs/PM_BOARD.md` untouched per scope. Nothing 🤖 for the bench from this
change — it only removes rows from its queue.

computed once with `curl | tr -d '\r' | sha256sum` and once from inside the Foundry page with
`crypto.subtle`). That is `main` @ `1c52cfd`, i.e. **item 84's ENGINE-ONLY fix is live**. World `edha`,
system 2.1.0, `edha-content` active, `Bench` GM, canvas ready on *Playtest Map*. **This run owes Ben
no deploy** — it changed no engine source and no authored data.

A second idempotency run was **not** made and the reason is on the record: the first run created

### ✅ Ten 🤖 rows retired on evidence (open 🤖 **20 → 10**; open ⚑ unchanged at 10)

**1. `34c` weapon-borne riders survive — item 84's live re-test, the run's headline.** Two different
weapons on two different adversaries, **neither carrying the `adversaryTalent` flag** run 42's mutation
had to add: `B43 Surecat` / *The Pounce Already Taken* (flags `{"edha-content":{"adversary":"Surecat"}}`,
`adversaryTalent` **null**) → *"⏰ The Pounce Already Taken (B43 Surecat): … **(hit Bench Target —
Adjacent A.)**"*; `B43 Fellstag` / *Antler Sweep* → *"⏰ Antler Sweep (B43 Fellstag): … **(hit Bench
Target — Adjacent B.)**"*. Both whispered to Bench + Gamemaster. Two weapons rules out "that one
weapon" and leaves the predicate, which is what changed.

**2. `34b` natural weapon not listed — the body half, from a real player client.** `PlayerBench`
(`isGM: false`) owning `Bench — Order` at a measured **5.0 ft** from a defeated `B43 Cinderhound`:
double-click → *"Edha: Cinderhound — nothing worth taking."*, no card. **Positive control, same body,
same click, one minute later:** a plain `Sidesword` (`alwaysEquipped: false`) added from the GM client
made the identical double-click post *"🎒 Searching Cinderhound — take: **Sidesword**"*, authored by
PlayerBench, whispered to PlayerBench + Bench + Gamemaster — the Sidesword listed, the `alwaysEquipped`
Bite not. Plumbing and exclusion proven in one window.

**3+4. R-64 ×2 — both "no drivable shape" verdicts overturned, by the staged-clone escape.** Runs 25/29
proved `edha-focus`, `edha-next-test-mod` and `edha-cae-grant`, and recorded `edha-reveal` and
`edha-owner-list` as unreachable because every shipped rule of those two sits on an event that resolves
its own target. **That is a statement about the data, not the harness.** Sharp Eye's
`edha-reveal {target: victim}` and Cold Eyes' `edha-owner-list {target: victim}` were copied onto ONE
scratch talent on `Bench — Heroic` with their event moved to **`edha-on-hit`** (and the list rule's `op`
set to `place`). `Bench — Heroic` then hit **Adjacent A** while `game.user.targets` held **Adjacent B**
for the whole window: *"👁️ … on **Bench Target — Adjacent A** …"* and *"📋 … **Bench Target — Adjacent
A** bears your **Quarry** (1/2)."*, with the ledger holding `Actor.8A0f88mKYTfmLzDb` = A and **Adjacent
B receiving nothing**. ⭐ Seven shipped talents corroborated the chain for free in the same take
(Feinting Strike's drain *and* its CAE burn, Startling Blow, Shattering Blow, Subtle Takedown, Meteoric
Leap, Anatomical Insight). ⚠️ Recorded as the trap: the *first* attempt — switching the canvas target
while Sharp Eye's roll dialog was open — moved the **def-test itself** onto B (*"Sharp Eye: 22 vs Bench
Target — Adjacent B's COG 14 — SUCCESS"*), so it discriminated nothing. ⚠️ Also corrected: the sweep
re-derived from the DEPLOYED packs finds **19** `edha-owner-list {target: victim}` rules, not run 29's
8 (12 on `edha-test-success`, **5 on `use`**, 2 on `edha-test-fail`) — the old sweep read the overlay.

**5. R-63 same-side checks — the third shape, as a matched control.** `Shatter Focus`'s reroll prompt
is gated on `edhaDisposHostile(owner, foe)`. Two creatures staged **identically** (`omen` status +
`markedBy.omen` pointing at a tokened `Bench — Chaos`), differing only in disposition, each rolling the
same ATH test: the **−1** enemy fired *"🩸 Shatter Focus — Bench Target — Adjacent A (your Omen-bearer)
just rolled a test (kept total 14). React? …"*; the **+1** ally produced **no Shatter Focus prompt at
all**. With run 31's enemies-in-range filter and run 42's Mantle aura (both directions), that is three
of the row's "2–3".

**6. R-61 legacy `detonateUsed` fallback — the row said it was untestable; it is not.** A staged legacy
flag is byte-identical to a real one, and the guard ORDER makes the answer unambiguous
(`edhaSceneOnceUsed` is checked before `requireNonEmpty`). `Bench — Destruction` / *The Unmooring*,
`sceneOnce` unset throughout: **CONTROL** (no flags) → *"Edha: The Unmooring — **no active markers to
detonate** (nothing spent)."*; **TEST** (only `flags.edha-content.detonateUsed.<itemId> = true`) → the
identical use → *"Edha: The Unmooring **is once per scene** — nothing spent."*

**7. CAE burns — both named talents, both wordings, with a before/after on the tracker.** CAE 1.3.1 is
live; a bench combat gave Adjacent A `reactionsAvailable [{max:1, remaining:1, used:0}]`. *Feinting
Strike* (on-hit, `target: victim`) → *"⚡ … loses one Reaction **(on the tracker)**."*, flag →
`{remaining: 0, used: 1}`. *Tactical Ploy* (test-success, `target: target`, *"18 vs … COG 14 —
SUCCESS"*) after resetting the group → same card, same decrement. The **no-combat fallback** was caught
earlier in the same session: *"… **(no tracker in this scene — honour-system)**."* ⚠️ The row quoted
*"burned on the tracker"*; the engine prints **"(on the tracker)"** — the row was written from the
design note, not the string.

**8. Kindle's token-light half — driven exactly as written, including the 30-second wait.** Adjacent A's
token before: `light {dim: 0, bright: 0, color: null, animation.type: null}`, no flags. `Bench — Red`
rolled Searing Bolt (**`2d8 + (5)[Kindle] + 5`** — the label half re-confirmed on the same card), **37 s**
elapsed, then the damage was applied with **no explicit source**, i.e. through the 120-s
`_edhaLastDealer` breadcrumb the GM's Apply click uses. After: **`{dim: 5, bright: 2.5, color:
"#ff7a1a", alpha: 0.5, animation {type: "flame", speed: 2, intensity: 2}}`** plus `kindleLit` and
`kindleLightPrev`. Restored from `kindleLightPrev`.

**9. Reckless Advance / Unstoppable — the fast-turn half, with its once/turn negative.** `B43 Brandram`
(2×2, `walk.rate` override 40) on a `testCollision`-clear lane, in an `active: false` viewed combat with
`turnSpeed: "fast"`: `Ram.rollDamage()` → *"💨 Unstoppable — B43 Brandram moves **20 ft** toward Bench
Target — Isolated, ignoring Reactions."* and the token really moved **(3600,4800) → (4800,4800) = 1200 px
= exactly 20.0 ft**, `oncePerTurn.Unstoppable` stamped. **NEG:** a second `Ram.rollDamage()` the same
turn → damage card only, **0 px**, flag unchanged. (Run 29 had already measured this half; run 42's
closing line overlooked its own row. Re-driven from scratch rather than argued.)

**10. Pyre spread card BY ALIAS — with the cross-owner control inside one combat.** A `B43 Cinderbrock`
*Fire the Wrack* Region (`sourceItem: "Fire the Wrack"`, `spreads: true`, its own `terrain.ownerUuid`)
and a `Bench — Destruction` *Pyre* Region sat in the same combat. End of the **Cinderbrock's** turn:
exactly one card, whispered to Bench + Gamemaster, labelled **Fire the Wrack** — not "Pyre" — and the
PC's zone silent. End of the **PC's** turn: exactly one card labelled **Pyre**, the Cinderbrock's zone
silent. **Both buttons work:** *Spread* → the GM prompt → a synthetic canvas click at scene (3150, 7350)
→ the Region gained a 300×300 shape at exactly **(3000, 7200)** and posted *"🔥 Fire the Wrack spreads
one square."*; *Extinguish* on each → *"💨 … is put out."* and both Regions deleted.

### ❌ / ⛔ Three rows annotated and left open, each with its blocker named

- **Predator's Due on-defeat — re-driven, heal PASSES, whisper still FAILS, and run 16's root cause is
  corrected.** Alpha **30 → 33**, card *"⚡ Predator's Due (B43 Cragdrake Alpha) — … regains **3**
  health … and 1 Focus on the kill (focus is a GM add). 2d8 2 1 3 3"* with **`whisper: []`**. It is not
  `edhaWhisperIds()` returning empty (that helper would have returned `[Bench, Gamemaster]`) — it is
  **`edhaRollCard` (`engine/33-triggered-effect-resolution.js:143`) passing no `whisper` key at all**,
  as do all seven of its call sites. **Blast radius: 17 adversary `edha-triggered-effect` rules**, of
  which the three `Predator's Due` blocks carry a literal GM instruction and the three `Afterburn`
  afflictions share the poster. → **item 88**, design call **R-90**.
- **Unbreakable Line (b) — re-confirmed against the DEPLOYED pack and filed.** Both blocks carry ONLY
  `edha-apply-watch → edha-gm-cue {rangeFt: 5}`; there is no `use` rule, so the White DC test its card
  promises has never existed. Same read found `The Reckoning`'s `activation.type` is **`"none"`** while
  the Crownox Ring's is `utility`. → **item 89** (REBUILD + ⟳ Sync when it lands).
- **R-62 audience flips — re-derived rather than inherited, and the blocker is now exact.** The world
  holds **exactly two GM users**, `Bench` and `Gamemaster`, both role 4; the other four are role 1. So
  `edhaGmIds({activeOnly: true})` and `edhaGmIds()` cannot differ during a bench run — there is no
  offline GM to discriminate with. Two exits, both outside a bench run: Ben disconnects for one window,
  or the row retires under **R-86**. → **R-91**.

### New rulings

- **R-90** — should an adversary's `edha-triggered-effect` card whisper to the GMs? *(default: (a)
  whisper via `edhaWhisperIds(owner)` when the owner has no player OWNER; leave player-owned cards
  public.)* Item 88 is blocked on it.
- **R-91** — does **R-86** retire the R-62 audience row, or do you want to disconnect `Gamemaster` for
  one deliberate window? *(default: (a) retire under R-86.)*

### Read the cards we did not come for

- `Thorn Hedge`'s cue fired at a creature **62.5 ft** away and its card still ended *"(Bench —
  Destruction's turn starts **in range**.)"*. Not a bug: the rule is authored `rangeFt: 0`, which is the
  engine's "no range gate", and the parenthetical is a fixed string. Recorded, not filed — but a GM
  reading that card is being told something the rule never checked.
- `edha-place-hazard` places the zone on the **current target's square** when a target is held; the
  click prompt is the fallback. Two zones landed on `Bench Target — Floater` before that was understood
  (both moved off and the three splashed actors healed back to their exact start HP).

### World diff — EMPTY

End-of-run per-actor comparison against the start snapshot (items, item names, HP, effect names,
statuses, `edha-content` flags, ownership): **no differences across all 74 actors.** Six imported `B43`
actors, nine tokens and two combats created and all deleted; both Regions extinguished by the row that
made them; scenes back to 33 / 30 tokens, 117 / 117 walls, 0 / 2 drawings, 1 / 0 regions, 0 templates,
19 lights each; 0 combats, 42 macros, 1 journal, folders identical. Three residues were found by the
diff and restored by hand: a `Surprised` effect + `bpHits` + a Tactical Ploy `nextTestMod` on Adjacent
A, a `Vigilant Stance` effect + `bpHits` on Bench — Heroic (Practiced Kata entering it at combat start),
and a `rally` flag on Bench — Red. **Chat is +72 messages** — Ben may flush bench chat. Both `Bench` and
`PlayerBench` logged out and confirmed selectable on `/join`; `Gamemaster` untouched throughout.

## 2026-09-07 — Item 87: the phone card's DEFAULT no longer reads empty for the bare `*Recommended*` style (TOOLING-only)

`RULING_DEFAULT_RE` in `scripts/build-dashboard.js` only matched the italic `*Recommended
default: …*` / `*Recommended: …*` COLON form, so a ruling whose recommendation is written in the
bare style — the marker attached to a lettered option instead of leading its own sentence, e.g.
R-56's "**(a)** extend the Edha table to adversary sheets AND their token sight, so one rule
governs everything — *Recommended*, and it matches the 07-17c ruling …" — fell through to
`default: "no default stated"` on the phone's "Needs you" card, even though a recommendation
exists. Three rulings use the bare style in `EDHA_RULINGS.md`; only R-56 is currently open (R-54
and R-57, the other two, are both ANSWERED/SHIPPED and never reach the card).

Added `bareRecommendedDefault()`: when the colon-form regex does not match, find the `*Recommended*`
marker, walk back to the nearest preceding `**(x)**` option label, and read forward to the next
option's label (or the end of the paragraph) — that span is the recommendation, markers stripped
the same way the colon-form path strips them. Measured against the real `EDHA_RULINGS.md`: R-83 /
R-88 / R-89's defaults are byte-identical before and after; R-56 is the only one that changes,
from `"no default stated"` to its real option-(a) sentence. Pinned against the real doc (R-56) and
a synthetic fixture (bare form + a ruling with no recommendation at all, which still correctly
reads `"no default stated"`); mutation-verified — reverting the fallback fails both new pins.
`node scripts/build-dashboard.js` rebuilt and committed. `EDHA_RULINGS.md` itself is untouched —
see the PR's open questions for whether the bare style should be normalised to the colon form
instead.

## 2026-09-07 — Item 85: a REOPENED ruling can resurface on the phone again; the Art tab's stray-`*` bold garble is fixed (TOOLING-only)

**Two bugs in the shared dashboard-build tooling, both in `scripts/build-dashboard.js` /
`scripts/lib/md.js`, neither touching the engine or a pack.**

`parseOpenRulings()` decided a ruling was CLOSED by scanning its whole body for ANY
`**ANSWERED …**` / `**VETOED …**` / `**SETTLED …**` marker, so R-56 — ANSWERED 2026-09-06, shipped,
then `**REOPENED 2026-09-07 (Ben, dashboard)…**` against its own answer (item 79's close-out) —
kept reading as closed: the older ANSWERED marker still matched, so the phone's "Needs you" cards
and the dash index's `openRulings` never surfaced it, even though Ben was waiting to be asked.
Fixed to take the LAST status marker in document order (a REOPENED after a closing marker reopens
it, a later closing marker after THAT re-closes it). Measured against the real `EDHA_RULINGS.md`:
open-ruling ids go from `["R-83","R-88","R-89"]` to `["R-56","R-83","R-88","R-89"]` — R-56 only.

Separately, the shared `inline()` renderer (`scripts/lib/md.js`) converted backtick code spans to
`<code>` BEFORE the `**bold**` pass, so a literal `*` inside a span (an art-wishlist heading's
`` `name-portrait.*` ``) survived as a bare asterisk that the bold regex could pair with an
unrelated `*`/`**` elsewhere in the string — garbling the Corvaine Raider and Mistheron Art tab
entries. Fixed by extracting code spans to a placeholder before bold/italic/strike run, restoring
them last. Both fixes are pinned (real-doc + synthetic tests for the rulings fix, one synthetic
case for the code-span fix); mutation-verified — reverting either file fails its new test(s).
`node scripts/build-dashboard.js` rebuilt and committed.

## 2026-09-07 — Item 84: weapon-borne `edha-on-hit` rules now dispatch — `edhaRulesForEvent` gates on `edhaRuleBearer` (ENGINE-ONLY, F5)

**The half of item 34a that got left behind.** When the fleet weapon migration moved the adversaries'
gear and natural attacks onto weapon-type documents, it widened the two actor-wide harvest loops
(`edhaActorRuleOf` / `edhaActorRulesOf`) from `edhaIsTalent` to `edhaRuleBearer` — talents **plus**
weapons. It did not widen `edhaRulesForEvent`, the selection all four *event* dispatchers read, so
that one kept excluding weapons by design. Result: **every `edha-on-hit` rule the migration put on a
weapon was silently inert** — six shipped rules, all `edha-on-hit → edha-gm-cue` (Wake-Eel Shoal /
*Worry the Failing*, Dirgehound Pack / *Worry the Straggler*, Callthief / *Take the Answerer*,
Surecat / *The Pounce Already Taken*, Fellstag / *Antler Sweep*, Keelshadow / *Breach and Drag*).

**Found by bench run 42**, not by a gate: Surecat's Pounce posted nothing on four applied hits, and
flipping `adversaryTalent = true` on that same weapon — the only field that changes `edhaIsTalent`'s
verdict — made the identical take post its cue. Brandram's Shockwave Slam (same event, on a *trait*)
was the positive control. **The fix is the one predicate swap** the bench's root-cause named:
`module-src/scripts/engine/04-black-ritual.js` now reads `if (!edhaRuleBearer(item)) continue;`.

**Proven by mutation, not by reading.** Two pins went into `tests/engine-helpers.test.js` BEFORE the
fix and failed on the shipped engine (`0 !== 1`, "weapon-borne edha-on-hit rule was dropped by the
selection"); after the swap the suite is **1021 passed, 0 failed** (was 1019/2). The block's old
`skips non-talent items entirely` case used a *weapon* as its decoy — precisely the behaviour that
was wrong — so its decoy is now plain `equipment`, matching what item 34a did to
`tests/actor-rules-of.test.js`.

**Verdict on the other three callers, all widened by the same swap and all provably inert for shipped
data** (0 weapon-borne rules of each in `data/`, counted across all five packs): `edha-combat-timing`
and `edha-ritual-paid` and `edha-draw-mana` each dropped weapon-borne rules identically and now do
not — pinned by a second test that loops all three events. **Not changed, on purpose:** the
`preUseItem` veto (`04-black-ritual.js`) keeps `edhaIsTalent`, because weapons deliberately carry no
talent-*use* automation — that exclusion is what `edhaRuleBearer`'s own comment documents. Two
adjacent `edhaIsTalent` harvest loops are reported to the PM rather than fixed here (out of scope,
0 shipped consumers each): `edhaListPlaceNotes` (`07-edha-owner-list.js`) drops a weapon-borne
`placeNote`, and `edhaCovBuffTemplate` (`49-order.js`) harvests ActiveEffects, not event rules.

**ENGINE-ONLY — F5 or relaunch, no pack rebuild and no ⟳ Sync.** The 13 `edha-pre-deal-damage →
edha-damage-rider` weapon rules were never affected (they go through the already-widened loops; four
measured firing at run 42), and no authored data changed. 🤖 **For bench run 43:** the 34c
*weapon-borne riders survive* row's **cue half** — Surecat's Pounce on an applied hit, with no
`adversaryTalent` flag this time, plus one more of the five (Fellstag / *Antler Sweep*) to prove it
is the predicate and not that one weapon. The row's rider half is already retired on run 42's
evidence. `node scripts/gates.js`: 11/11 PASS.

Surecat's `The Pounce Already Taken` carries `edha-on-hit → edha-gm-cue` **on the weapon document** and

`edhaActorRulesOf` to `edhaRuleBearer` (talents + weapons) and **left this one behind**.

Surecat/The Pounce Already Taken, Fellstag/Antler Sweep, Keelshadow/Breach and Drag. The 13

### Blocked / partial, with the blocker named

- **I10b2-1…4 and 77-1's unset-side clause — BLOCKED, blocker RE-DERIVED rather than inherited.** Creating a token with `disposition: null`, and updating one to `null` / `undefined` / `NaN`, all read back **−1 (HOSTILE)** on this build, so no sideless token can be staged; an actor with no token is in no range sweep either, so the row's own fallback discriminates nothing. `tests/disposition-failclosed.test.js` stays the proof. **77-1's aura halves both PASS live**: `affects: allies` → ally `1d20 + 4 + 1[Mantle of the Aspirant]`, enemy `1d20 + 0`; flipped to `affects: enemies` on the Events tab → enemy `1d20 + 0 + 1[Mantle…]`, ally `1d20 + 4`; rule restored.
- **34b's body half** — the deploy blocker is gone and the refusal is now genuinely exercised (`edhaLootableItems` returns `[]` for the Cinderhound's **body** and `["Bite"]` for a **cache**; a defeated Raider's body now offers **both** gear weapons where run 40 saw only the Shortsword) — but `edhaLootTryOpen` returns early for a GM by design, so the live card needs `PlayerBench`.
- **R-52 (c)(ii) MEASURED** (item 62's numbers): with a real 3×3 token and a touching 1×1 neighbour (edge-to-edge **0.0 ft** in every case), centre-to-centre is **1×1 5.0 · 2×2 7.5 · 3×3 10.0 · 3×3 diagonal 14.142 · 4×4 12.5 ft**. Against the current gate (c2c ≤ rule + 2.5 ft) the half-square slack fixes Large and nothing larger; the slack a Huge owner needs is 5 ft and a Gargantuan 7.5 — i.e. size-dependent, which is what edge-to-edge gives for free.

### Two new rulings, and Ben's own 13 marks

**R-88** (Volatile Strike still carries `whenDamageType: "impact"`, so a keen sword hit offers nothing
while the card promises "any melee attack") and **R-89** (the `NO NAMEABLE HOOK` comment **does not
survive** a ProseMirror round-trip — measured through `parseString`/`serializeString`, the pair the
editor uses on save, so a Foundry edit + extract would silently drop the marker and fail lint pass 5).
Also applied on this pass: Ben's **13 dashboard marks** of 2026-09-07 (11 ⚑ + Job 6a + the sight-range
design note), each row flipped with his note verbatim; the GM-less / two-GM rows cite **R-86**.

### Harness lessons (both cost this run real time — they are in the runbook)

⭐ **With the Browser pane hidden the PIXI ticker does not run, so v13 animated token movement never
completes and the document position never commits.** Every engine slide (`edha-push`, `edha-move`,
charges, leaps) reported its distance on the card and moved **0 px** until the take was followed by a
`canvas.app.ticker.update()` pump; with the pump the same push landed 599.7 px. A `{teleport: true}`
update was never affected, which is why earlier runs' staging worked and their *measurements* didn't.
⚠️ **A second token of the same actor makes an engine result unreadable** — a duplicate `Bench — Order`
token sent Shockwave Slam's push to the wrong copy and printed *"stopped by Bench Target — Adjacent B"*,
a token 8,400 px away, which reads exactly like an engine bug. Hard rule 7, one level further: don't
just resolve by id, don't *create* the ambiguity.

## 2026-09-06 — FIX PASS 10 (TODO 78): the doubled rider flavor, fixed at the MESSAGE instead of at the render — and the R-71 fold extended to the rider half (**ENGINE-ONLY → F5**; no pack rebuild, no ⟳ Sync)

Bench run 41's one FAIL. Fix pass 9 (TODO 72) repaired the string correctly and repaired it in a
place the card never reads, so Ambush Bite's bar still read
`1d10 + 3 + (1d6[Ambush Bite])[Ambush Bite] + 0` on the hash-verified `f2fb3e2d…` engine.

### Bug root causes (the one FAIL row, re-derived from the installed sources, not from the report)

- **ROOT CAUSE — a Roll carries TWO formula strings, and the chat round-trip makes them disagree.**
  Run 41 was right that the doubling "lives in the message data" and right that another
  `renderChatMessageHTML` registration cannot fix it; the mechanism underneath is more specific than
  "the system re-renders asynchronously", and it is what decides where the repair belongs.
  - `roll._formula` is the string the roll was BUILT from — stored by `toJSON()` and rebuilt by
    `Roll.fromData`'s `new this(data.formula, …)` (`client/dice/roll.mjs:40`, `:1055`). **Core's own
    chat template prints this one** (`roll.mjs:892`).
  - `roll.formula` is a GETTER that recompiles from the terms on every read (`roll.mjs:173` →
    `Roll.getFormula`, `:563`). **The cosmere damage card prints this one** — `enrichDamage`,
    `partsNormal.push(rollNormal.formula)`.
  - Between them: `ParentheticalTerm#_evaluate` calls `roll.propagateFlavor(this.flavor)`, stamping
    the parenthetical's flavor onto every inner term that has none (`parenthetical.mjs:105` →
    `roll.mjs:496`); the constructor then re-derives `this.term = roll.formula` whenever a roll is
    supplied (`parenthetical.mjs:19`), which is exactly what `_fromData` does when the message is
    read back. So the STORED data is clean and the REHYDRATED term is not.
  That is precisely the split run 41 measured and could not explain: its probe saw the single-labelled
  `_formula` at hook time (core's render) and the doubled getter 1 500 ms later (the system's). The
  1 500 ms is not a timer — the system's `getHTML` calls `super.getHTML()` (which fires
  `renderChatMessageHTML`) and only THEN runs `enrichCardContent`, which **replaces
  `.message-content` wholesale**. The display tidy therefore walks a node the system is about to
  throw away, on every damage card, forever.
- **THE FIX — repair the message, not the render.** New pure helper `edhaUndoFlavorPropagation`
  (`01-shared-core.js`) strips, from a serialized roll, the flavor copies equal to the enclosing
  parenthetical's own flavor, bound on **`preCreateChatMessage`** (the first and only registration of
  that hook in the engine; no second render registration was added). `propagateFlavor` uses `??=`, so
  a term that carried its OWN flavor is never a propagated copy and is left alone. The rehydrated
  `term` then re-derives as the engine wrote it, and the two strings agree on every client and every
  later render. **The parentheses stay in the roll** — the system's graze clone keeps only
  DiceTerm/OperatorTerm/PoolTerm (`filterTermsSafely`, the `@damage.dice` path), so a bare rider die
  would start riding grazes. **Fix pass 9's expected string was therefore wrong to drop them**: the
  correct bar is `1d10 + 3 + (1d6)[Ambush Bite] + 0`, and the checklist row now says so.
- **`edhaTidyFormula` KEPT, with its job restated.** It still repairs messages already in Ben's log
  and any doubled string that reaches a surface it can see; it is no longer the fix for this card.
  Its pass-9 pins are untouched and green.
- **SECOND root cause, same three lines — the R-71 fold never reached the rider half.** Run 41 read
  Prognosis as `2d8 + 2 + ((2)d(2 * 3 + 2))[Prognosis]`: item 69 folds the BASE damage formula in
  `edhaWrapRollDamage`, but `edhaRiderBonus` wrapped each rider's `bonusFormula` RAW, so one bar
  carried a folded base and an unfolded rider. Judgment asked for in the brief: **it belongs here**,
  not in a separate item — same handler, same wrapper, same card, and R-71 already settled that the
  fold is the right treatment; the rider half was simply missed. Fixed with `edhaFoldRiderFormula`.
  **Its safety property is the load-bearing part:** the rider resolves here against the ROLLER's data
  while the system resolves it a moment later against `getDamageRollData`
  (`{...actor.getRollData(), mod, skill, attribute, source}`). Substituting with **no `missing`**
  leaves an unresolved `@ref` in place, so a rider needing one of those four extra keys is DETECTED
  and handed on raw, exactly as before. Every `edha-damage-rider` `bonusFormula` in the atlas today
  references only `@tier` / `@skills.*` / a literal (surveyed). The fold is **not** applied in
  `edhaRiderParts`: the burst executor evaluates the parts numerically and wants them raw.

### New REUSABLE primitives

- **`edhaUndoFlavorPropagation(rollData)`** + its `preCreateChatMessage` binding — PURE. Any future
  labelled-parenthetical term gets a stable round-trip for free.
- **`edhaFoldRiderFormula(formula, rollData)`** — PURE. R-71's fold with the unresolved-`@ref`
  bail-out, reusable by anything that wants to pre-resolve an authored formula for DISPLAY without
  risking a substitution the system would have done better.

Both indexed in `ENGINE_INDEX.md`, including the two-formula-strings fact — read that entry before
touching formula-bar display code again.

### Pins (iron rule 4)

`tests/roll-flavor-propagation.test.js` (new, 10 cases) models core's rehydration from the two
getters above, so it **reproduces the exact bench-run-41 string from serialized data** and then shows
it clean; it covers the compound rider, the hook wiring, idempotence, a malformed entry, and two
negative controls (a differently-labelled inner term, an unflavored parenthetical).
`tests/fold-die-math.test.js` gains four rider-fold cases including the `@mod`/`@skill`/`@attribute`
bail-out. **Verified by mutation, not by reading:** neutering the strip loop fails 6 cases; removing
the `preCreateChatMessage` registration fails 3. 1 007 tests pass; all 11 gates PASS.

### Known limits

- **Not verifiable from here — 🤖, two rows** in the engine-wide section plus the reworded Ambush
  Bite row in the Black block. The bench must read the `.dice-formula` node ~2 s after the card
  lands (run 41's own lesson), not `msg.rolls[0].formula`.
- **The trailing `+ 0`** is the system's own `+ ${rollData.mod}` (`rollDamage`), not ours, and is
  left alone. **Messages already in the chat log** keep their doubled data; only `edhaTidyFormula`
  touches those, and only where it can see them.

### ❌ FAIL (1) → test-pass-fixes: Ambush Bite's doubled rider flavor is NOT fixed on the card

The row stays open with the observation appended. **The repair is correct and it runs in the wrong
place.**

- **Measured.** One fooled Ambush Bite from a fresh `Stillback` import (Perception 7 vs DC 12 →
  *"taken in"*, the belief rolled for real): the chat card's `.dice-formula` reads
  **`1d10 + 3 + (1d6[Ambush Bite])[Ambush Bite] + 0`** — the doubled label the fix was supposed to
  remove. The damage maths is right (dice 3 and 3, +3 = 9) and the **NEG passes**: the graze block
  is **`1d10`** only, no `1d6`.
- **Root cause, hook → handler → write.** `edhaTidyFormula` itself is fine — fed that exact string
  in the console it returns `1d10 + 3 + 1d6[Ambush Bite] + 0`. The problem is *when* it runs. A
  probe hook on `renderChatMessageHTML` for that message fired **twice**, both times on a
  **detached** element (`root.isConnected === false`), and in both firings the node already read
  **`1d10 + 3 + (1d6)[Ambush Bite] + 0`** — *single*-labelled, so the engine's own guard
  (`if (!inner.includes(tag)) return m;`) correctly left it alone. **1 500 ms later the same root is
  `isConnected === true` and reads the doubled string**, with the graze block now present and the
  node order changed: the system re-renders the damage section asynchronously, after the hook, and
  that rebuild is where `term = roll.formula` re-derives the inner label. There is **no further
  `renderChatMessageHTML` pass** on the connected element. The message's own `rolls[0].formula` is
  already the doubled string, so the doubling lives in the message data, not only in the DOM.
- **The discriminator, free, in the same session:** on an **engine-rolled** bare-roll message
  (Prognosis's rider on Life Surge) the tidy DID land — the DOM read
  `2d8 + 2 + ((2)d(2 * 3 + 2))[Prognosis]` while `rolls[0].formula` held
  `…[Prognosis])[Prognosis]`. So the display hook works wherever it gets to see the final string;
  the system's own damage **card** is the one surface it does not.
- **Where a fix probably belongs:** either upstream of the message (stop the doubled `term` being
  serialised) or on a render pass that sees the *connected* element. Another
  `renderChatMessageHTML` registration will not do it.

## 2026-09-06 — Item 76: the phone card's DEFAULT reads through bold-inline defaults; a bold APPLIED note marks a ruling applied wherever it lives (**TOOLING + DOCS-ONLY**; TODO_REPO_HYGIENE #76; PR #262)

**What changed.** `scripts/build-dashboard.js` `parseOpenRulings()`: `RULING_DEFAULT_RE` now
captures `(?:\*\*[^*]*\*\*|[^*])+` — the italic `*Recommended[ default]: …*` run read THROUGH its
inner `**…**` spans — and the caller strips the bold markers. New `RULING_APPLIED_MARK_RE`: a bold
span holding the upper-case word `APPLIED` marks the entry `applied: true` in any section, beside
the existing §I rule. `EDHA_RULINGS.md`: R-80 / R-81 bold their `APPLIED` and say "(stub in §I)";
§I gains a one-line stub for each in the R-84 / R-85 shape. No recommended default, heading, or
answered ruling changed.

**Why.** The old `[^*]+` stopped at the first `*` of `**(a) …**`, captured `""`, and — being a
match — also kept the "no default stated" fallback from firing, so six of the eight open rulings'
"Needs you" cards showed no default. And `applied` was decided by section alone, so R-48 / R-80 /
R-81 / R-84 / R-85 — all marked APPLIED in §C, with only a `RULING_STUB_RE`-skipped stub in §I —
rendered as ordinary default-ask cards instead of "applied — veto?" cards.

**Proven.** `pm-state.js --dashboard-dir`: all 8 open rulings carry a non-empty default; R-48 /
R-80 / R-81 / R-84 / R-85 `applied: true`, R-18 / R-82 / R-83 `false` (lower-case "not applied"
is deliberately not a mark). `tests/pm-state.test.js` pins the plain and bold-inline fixtures and
the real file; the old regex fails `R-80: default is empty … ""`, dropping the mark check fails
`R-80 carries a bold APPLIED note in §C`. `docs/pm-state.json` untouched. Nothing for the bench.

## 2026-09-06 — Item 44: `Ask:` lines on open rulings, and the parser that reads them (**DOCS-ONLY** + a two-line TOOLING change; TODO_REPO_HYGIENE #44; PR #258)

**What changed.** Phase 2 of item 43's phone "Needs you" view. Item 43's `parseOpenRulings()`
took a card's question from the ruling's **heading** only — there was no `Ask:` fallback to
coordinate with — so this item settled the format and taught the parser to read it: an `Ask:`
paragraph (ONE line, its own paragraph directly under the heading paragraph, `(a)/(b)` named
when the entry has them) now wins over the heading (`scripts/build-dashboard.js`,
`RULING_ASK_RE`). The convention is written into `EDHA_RULINGS.md`'s intro ("The phone card's
question"). One line on purpose: `parseRulings()` pushes each prose line as its own block, so a
wrapped `Ask:` would lose everything after its first line.

**The audit.** 8 open rulings (R-18, R-48, R-80 … R-85; open = not ANSWERED / VETOED / SETTLED,
§I and §J/§K excluded — R-73 is VETOED, so §I currently holds no open card). Four got an `Ask:`
line: **R-48** (the heading names one block; the ask now covers the run-19 family the UPDATE
widened it to), **R-81** (heading leaned on R-46), **R-82** (leaned on R-14), **R-83** ("three
`hea` writers", unnamed). Four already had a self-contained question heading and were left
alone: R-18, R-80, R-84, R-85. No heading reworded, no default changed, no answered ruling touched.

**Proven.** `node scripts/pm-state.js --dashboard-dir <scratch>` → `index.json.openRulings` yields
a `?`-terminated question on all 8; `tests/pm-state.test.js` pins the fixture (an `Ask:` replaces
a symptom heading; no `Ask:` → the heading stays) and the real file (R-81's ask names (a)/(b);
every open ask ends in `?`); with the parser change stashed, both pins fail. The tracked
`docs/pm-state.json` was NOT regenerated — `openRulings` rides in the dashboard index, not the
board state, so the projection did not change. Nothing 🤖 — no table involved.

**Found, not fixed.** `RULING_DEFAULT_RE` (`[^*]+` after `*Recommended default:`) yields an EMPTY
default for every entry whose default is written bold-inline (`*Recommended default: **(a) …**`) —
R-80 … R-85 all show `default: ""` on their cards, and the page's "no default stated" fallback
never triggers because the string is not missing, merely empty. Reported to the PM.

## 2026-09-06 — FIX PASS 9 (TODO 72): bench run 40's four defects — the dead `once` budget, the doubled rider flavor, R-85 and R-84 (**ENGINE-ONLY → F5 / relaunch**; no rebuild, no ⟳ Sync)

Four fixes, one per commit, all four root-caused against the **installed Foundry and cosmere
sources** before anything was changed — two of the four turned out to have a mechanism the bench's
(confident, and largely correct) report had not reached.

### Rulings (Ben, 2026-09-06 — both APPLIED as their recommended default, both vetoable)
- **R-84 — an offer that CANNOT be made refunds its Investiture, and the card names the money.**
  Applied as (a). One deviation, stated: the non-refundable line reads **"no cost was spent"**, not
  (b)'s *"the cost was spent"* — with R-17's gate, `refundable === false` on this branch means the
  offer came from a watch / success rule where the system charged **nothing** (that rule's `costs`
  land on the click, which never happens here), so "spent" would be a false statement to put on a
  card. A veto is a one-line revert.
- **R-85 — `expireEndOfRound` falls back to the BEARER's combat.** Applied as (a), verbatim
  (`edhaCombatRoundOf(owner) ?? edhaCombatRoundOf(target)`). A veto is a one-word revert.

### Bug root causes (one bullet per CAUSE, not per change)
- **The `edha-prompt-pick` `once` budget never bit — the third instance of the dotted-flag-key
  class, and the ledger the class's own doc had not swept.** `edhaPromptPickClick` marks with the
  talent's **UUID**; `ClientDatabaseBackend#_updateDocuments` runs `foundry.utils.expandObject` on
  **every** update (`client/data/client-backend.mjs:208`) and that helper **recurses into every plain
  object** (`common/utils/helpers.mjs:495`), so the document stored
  `coordRound.Actor.<id>.Item.<id>._pick` while the reader asked for the flat key and got
  `undefined` — for ever. `ENGINE_INDEX`'s "⛑ A DOTTED KEY IN A FLAG VALUE" section already existed,
  already named the escape (`edhaFlagKey`), and already listed which ledgers ran it: the
  ambush-belief ledger and `trigRound`. `coordRound` was the third and was not on the list. So the
  fix is the **existing** helper at both ends of the store, not a new one — nine call sites are safe
  now and the eight dot-free ones are byte-identical. **Blast radius, measured against the data:**
  exactly three `edha-prompt-pick` rules carry `once: "round"` — Unnerving Approach (Black), its
  adversary twin, and Puppeteer — and all three had a dead budget. `edhaCoordOPRAllowed` also keeps a
  one-time `foundry.utils.getProperty` fallback, so a document stamped tonight in the expanded shape
  still reads as spent for the rest of that round rather than handing back a free use across the F5.
- **The doubled rider flavor is FOUNDRY's, and the parentheses are load-bearing.** The bench filed
  `1d10 + 3 + (1d6[Ambush Bite])[Ambush Bite] + 0` as "something upstream already labelled it". It
  had not: `edhaRiderBonus` emits `(1d6)[Ambush Bite]` and item 66's `edhaJoinRiderTerm` was never on
  this path. The doubling is a two-step in the client: `ParentheticalTerm#_evaluateAsync` calls
  `roll.propagateFlavor(this.flavor)`, which stamps the parenthetical's flavor onto every inner term
  that has none (`client/dice/terms/parenthetical.mjs:105` → `client/dice/roll.mjs:496`), and
  `SERIALIZE_ATTRIBUTES = ["term", "roll"]` with a constructor that re-derives `this.term =
  roll.formula` — so the chat round-trip rebuilds the term string **with** the inner label and
  `expression` re-appends the outer one. That is exactly why the unparenthesised next-test join
  (`1d6 - 1d6[Probability Net] + 4`) printed clean in the same bench session. **The obvious fix —
  drop the parentheses — is wrong:** the system's graze clone keeps only DiceTerm / OperatorTerm /
  PoolTerm (`filterTermsSafely` on the `@damage.dice` path), so an unparenthesised rider die would
  start riding grazes, which is both a rules change and a re-run of the 07-17 graze-clone bug. The
  roll is right and only the display lies (§10's drift direction), so the repair lands in
  `edhaTidyFormula`, the display-only formula-bar layer that already owns this class. Family-wide:
  **every** `edha-damage-rider` with a `bonusFormula` printed doubled — Ambush Bite, Spearing Beak,
  Prognosis, Kindle, Momentum's Edge, Scalpel-Strike.
- **A `null` round stamp can never expire (R-85).** `edhaNextModExpired` requires `mod.round != null`
  before it prunes, and `edha-next-test-mod` stamped the GRANTER's combat — so a granter outside the
  tracker wrote `round: null` and a "this round" rider sat on the victim for ever. The fallback is
  the bearer's combat; with both sides out of combat the stamp is still null, which is the honest
  answer.
- **R-17's refund gate had a third case it never saw.** The `emptyNote` branch posted its note and
  returned, and a rule fired from the talent's own `use` has already been charged by the system, so
  the money left with nothing to show and no Decline button to ask for it back. Same gate
  (`edhaOfferRefundable`), same single path — `edhaOfferDecline` with a null msg credits and resolves
  nothing, so `edhaRefundCost` still has **exactly one caller** in the offer family and pin 4's
  invariant is unmoved. Three rules carry a `source: "creatures"` + `emptyNote` pair and all three
  are fixed: Unnerving Approach (Black + twin), Anticipate (Blue), Terms of Accord (White).

### New REUSABLE primitives
None — and that is the finding. Every fix reuses something already in the engine (`edhaFlagKey`,
`edhaTidyFormula`, `edhaOfferDecline`), which is why the pass is 87 lines of source across four
files. `ENGINE_INDEX`'s dotted-key section gains `coordRound` and the worked example of what missing
that escape costs.

### Audit wider than the report
- **Every `edhaCoordOPR*` caller** (nine): only `edhaPromptPickClick`'s two pass a dotted string;
  the rest pass talent names, item ids or literals, and **no name or id anywhere in `data/` contains
  a dot** (checked across `leyline.json`, `domain.json`, `cosmere.json`, `adversaries.json` — the one
  hit, `"Item name."`, is a schema doc-string, not a name). Fixed at the boundary anyway, so a future
  caller cannot re-create it.
- **Every dynamic object key written into a flag value**: three, and all three now escape
  (`ambushBelief.tested`, `trigRound`, `coordRound`). The only other dynamic-key store,
  `_edhaFocusFire.byTarget`, is in-memory and keyed by token id.
- **Every `edhaCombatRoundOf(owner)` stamp that could be null** (a dozen): `expireEndOfRound` was the
  only one where null means *never expires*. `castRound` (Sovereignty steps) compares
  `edhaCombatRoundOf(owner) !== pe.castRound`, so null-vs-null matches correctly; the offer flag's
  `round` is gated by `offer.round == null → skip` in the sweep, which is fail-closed (an out-of-combat
  offer waits for its Decline button, as documented); `edhaCoordOPR*` and `edhaTriggerAllowed` return
  early on null. Nothing else needed changing.
- **Not changed, deliberately:** `edha-prompt-pick`'s `source: "effects"` empty case, which posts a
  full offer card **with** the Decline button — R-17 already refunds that one.

### Verification
`node scripts/gates.js` PASS on every commit (11 gates, `engine-assembly` included). 12 new pinned
cases across `tests/coord-opr-key.test.js` (new), `tests/next-mod-round-fallback.test.js` (new),
`tests/engine-helpers.test.js` and `tests/offer-decline-refund.test.js`; each fix was shown FAILING
under a one-line reversion of itself, restored, and re-run green (984 → 984 tests, 0 failed). Item 4
landed mid-pass, so the four hunks are in their `module-src/scripts/engine/NN-*.js` sources and
`engine-assemble --check` is OK.

### 🤖 for the bench (run 41)
Four re-test rows: the `once` budget (three uses in one round, plus a read of the stored FLAT flag
shape), Ambush Bite's formula bar (`1d10 + 3 + 1d6[Ambush Bite]`, with the graze half as the negative
control), R-84's refund + card wording, and R-85's non-combatant grant expiring. Nothing here is ⚑ —
none of it is Ben's judgment except the two vetoes, which are filed in `EDHA_RULINGS.md`.

**What changed.** The repo's front door contradicted the reference (handoff §1/§7): `CLAUDE.md` still

backlog", iron rule 2b's ratchet clause presented the 2026-07-24 counts (90 / 200 / 75, the 221-name

**The new rule (iron rule 5, CLAUDE.md):** a dated delta goes at the **TOP of the current month's
file** in `docs/handoff-changelog/`, directly under its marker line — never into
`EDHA_FOUNDRY_HANDOFF.md`, which is now the cold-start REFERENCE and nothing else (691 lines, TOC
first). This is the first delta written in the new place.

**What moved where.** `scripts/handoff-split.js` (new, kept — idempotent, re-run after any merge
that lands a delta in the handoff on the old rule) cut the handoff at `## Reference — table of
contents` and bucketed every `## 2026-MM-DD …` block above it by month, verbatim, newest first:
`2026-06.md` 18 deltas, `2026-07.md` 42, `2026-08.md` 1, `2026-09.md` 81 — **142 headings before,

`8ae508b816236c9c1ab20d9c8378c4b1c8021dde37c1af8925422317389f42a5` = SHA-256 of the month files'
delta bodies concatenated in the same order (generated headers excluded), cross-checked with an
independent `sed | sha256sum` of the pre-move file. `HANDOFF_ARCHIVE.md` →

header-wall summaries, condensed duplicates of full deltas rather than deltas, so it moved whole
instead of being bucketed. `README.md` in the folder is the generated index.

file that still exists; follow only tracks renames). `git log -S"<delta title>" --
EDHA_FOUNDRY_HANDOFF.md` does — verified for a June, a July and a September title (89b9359 /

## 2026-09-06 — Item 24: the native event/handler registry is TABLE-DRIVEN — `EDHA_EVENT_TYPES` / `EDHA_HANDLER_TYPES`, one registration loop, schemas EVALUATED instead of regex-parsed (**ENGINE-ONLY → F5 / relaunch**; PR #244)

**What changed.** `edhaRegisterNativeEventSystem()` was ~2,500 lines of **102 sequential**
`api.registerItemEventType` / `api.registerItemEventHandlerType` calls (15 events + 87 handlers).
The definitions are now DATA: two top-level arrays in the one engine file (iron rule 2a), built
inside an IIFE so `FF`, `choices` and the deal-damage debounce map stay private, and registered by
**one loop** — `for (const def of EDHA_EVENT_TYPES) api.registerItemEventType(def)` and the handler
twin. Registration sites **102 → 1 loop**. Same order, same labels, descriptions, hooks and schema
fields, and the SAME objects: the system binds an executor's `this` via
`executor.call(this, event)` on the handler DataModel, so each array element is the former call's
argument untouched and every closure / `this` binding is preserved. Both tables sit on the `edha`
API (`edha.EDHA_HANDLER_TYPES`, beside `createLootCache`). The region-behaviour registration and the
API guard in the function are untouched.

**The tooling half.** `scripts/handler-schemas.js` used to regex-parse the engine SOURCE to
recover each handler's field schema (lint pass 9/9b, the item-64 build guard). It now EVALUATES the
tables: `tests/harness.js` gained a `foundry.data.fields` stub and `loadHandlerRegistry()`, which
runs the registration against a recording API and hands back the very `config.schema` objects
Foundry receives. `parseHandlerSchemas(src)` / `parseHandlerChoices(src)` keep their signatures
(`src` ignored; memoized). lint-refs **pass 18** had its own source regex for `(type, hook)` — it
reads the registry now too. `matchBrace` / `topLevelKeys` stay for `dump-native-vocabulary.js`,
which parses the SYSTEM bundle we cannot evaluate. Parity on the pre-refactor engine: old parser
vs evaluated registry produce identical schema and choices maps.

**Proven, not asserted.** (1) `tests/fixtures/handler-registry.snapshot.json` — per event
`{type, label, description, hook}`, per handler `{type, label, description, fields[]}` in
declaration order — was generated from the OLD engine and **committed before the refactor**
(`cf15416`); `tests/handler-registry.test.js` holds the engine to it byte-for-byte and the fixture is
untouched by the refactor commits. (2) Mutation: dropping the `edha-multi-hit` row fails with
`handlers: registered no more — edha-multi-hit (a dropped table row?)` (and lint pass 9 flags
Flashpoint's rule as unregistered); restored → 966/966. (3) Pins: exactly one
`registerItemEventHandlerType(` and one `registerItemEventType(` in stripped code; the table
elements are `===` the recorded registrations; the API literal names both tables. `gates.js` 10/10.

**🤖 for the bench** — `# BENCH — Handler registry smoke (item 24)`: the Events-tab picker still
offers all 87 `edha-*` handler types (and 15 events), and one talent per handler family still fires.
Nothing here is Ben's judgment.

## 2026-09-06 DELTA — Item 68 / fix pass 8: a heal card states what was DELIVERED, not what was rolled (**ENGINE-only → F5**)

Bench run 39, driving R-10's load-bearing negative through a real Withering Touch mark, measured
this pair on one creature in one window: HP **4 → 4** (correct — the mark blocks it), the gate's own
card *"🩸 B39 Victim cannot regain HP (Withering Touch)"* (correct), and then
*"⚕️ Field Medicine: B39 Victim heals **5**."* The write was right and the card lied, one line under
the card that said why. That is the §10 drift direction that costs a table the most: **the card is
the only thing the players read.**

### Bug root cause — ONE contract, seven announcers

`edhaCrossHeal` scales its write through `edhaHealCutGate` and **returned nothing**. So every caller
that announced a heal had exactly one number available — the roll — and used it. That is not a
Field Medicine bug; it is every rule-driven heal card in the engine, and each one misreports a
BLOCKED heal *and* a HALVED one (naming the full amount where half landed). The reported row is
simply the arm the bench happened to drive.

Fixed at the contract, in one shape:

- **`edhaCrossHeal` now RETURNS the delivered amount** — the gated number on the owned leg and the
  relayed leg alike, `0` when the mark blocked it. The drop-to-1 bypass still reports its full
  amount. **No new `edhaHealCutGate` call site**: this reads the mark through `edhaHealCutInfo`, so
  R-10's family count of exactly two gate calls is untouched (`tests/drop-to-one-family.test.js`
  turns on that count and still passes).
- **`edhaHealLine(who, requested, delivered, phrase)`** is the one place that decides whether a
  number may be printed at all. `phrase(delivered)` is only ever called with a number that landed —
  so a halved mark simply reaches it with the halved value — and when the gate zeroed it the amount
  is **never** printed: the clause names the mark, in the gate's own words. Clauses come back
  unpunctuated so a caller can compose them.
- **All seven announcers wired**: H10's `hea` arm (Field Medicine — the reported row), Interposing
  Shield's `heal-ally` button, Shared Burden's `redirect` button (the owner's damage number is
  measured and unchanged; the note now says when less of it came back off the victim), the
  triggered-effect heal (which already gated but still printed a bare `0`), the Life regen tick, the
  regrowth tick, and Lifeline's intercept card.
- **The pulse sweep** is the one GROUP card, and a group is where a single number cannot be true for
  everyone — each creature carries its own mark. It counted reach and quoted the per-target roll
  (*"healed 3 of 3 ally(ies) 4 HP"* when two of the three got nothing). It now counts who was
  **healed**, totals what **landed**, and names whoever the mark stopped.
- **The same drift one arm over.** TODO 68 asked for the `inv` / `foc` arms to be audited beside
  `hea`. `foc` was already honest — `edhaGainFocus`/`edhaDrainFocus` announce `next - cur` and go
  silent when nothing moved, which is the precedent this pass generalizes. `inv` announced the
  rolled `n` against a **clamped** write, so a gain onto a nearly-full pool overstated by whatever
  the clamp ate; it now reports the delta and stays silent at 0.

### Proof

`tests/heal-announce-delivered.test.js` drives the **shipped** executor: H10's `hea` arm lives
inside a `registerItemEventHandlerType` config object, so the file registers the native event system
against a recording api stub and calls the real executor with the real config — the same two cards
Ben would read, in the same order. Bench 39's take is reproduced exactly (HP 4 → 4, gate card, then
the talent's card), plus the halved case (`heals 2`, HP 4 → 6), the untouched no-mark case
(`heals 5`), the regrowth / Life-regen / pulse announcers, the Investiture clamp, and the
`edhaCrossHeal` return contract on both legs. **Mutations:** build the card from `n` again → the
blocked and halved cases fail; give `edhaCrossHeal` back its bare `return` → seven cases fail across
every announcer.

### Found in passing — NOT fixed here (needs a ruling, not a commit)

`ENGINE_INDEX.md` says *"any heal path that writes `hea` outside applyDamage MUST call the gate."*
Three do not: **`edha-regen`**'s turn-end write, the **decay lifesteal** heal-back, and
**`edhaBurstDetonate`**'s heal hits (which reach `edhaApplyBurstResults`, and that helper must stay
ungated — Raise Dead's stabilizing 1 HP rides it, R-10). Their cards are honest about what they
deliver, so item 68 does not touch them; but closing the gap changes **live HP at the table** (a
withered creature would stop being healed by Mending Aura), which is an R-10-adjacent decision. It
also needs the family test's gate-call count raised from 2 with a declaration. **Filed for the
board, not decided here.** The decay lifesteal separately announces its pre-clamp `back` where the
write is `min(max, cur + back)` — the same over-announcement the `inv` arm just lost.

### New REUSABLE primitive

- **`edhaHealLine(who, requested, delivered, phrase)`** — see `ENGINE_INDEX.md`. Any future heal
  card joins the contract by calling it; the pinned family test counts the call sites, so a new
  announcer that skips it is caught.

## 2026-09-06 — Item 56: the melee mutation riders follow their OWN card's graze wording, R-14 (**ENGINE + AUTHORED → deity pack REBUILD + ⟳ Sync**, Ben only; the engine half alone is F5)

R-14 (Ben, (c) "follow each rider's own card"): "on a hit" = hit only; "when you deal damage" /
"on a hit or graze" = grazes count. Root cause of the old behaviour: the system decides hit-vs-graze
on the CHAT MESSAGE (`CosmereChatMessage#useGraze`, the card's subtotal toggle) and calls
`actor.applyDamage(instances, { originatingItem })` with no marker, so the three riders that ride a
buffed creature's own application — Bone Spurs (+keen, pre-pass), Venom Glands (Afflicted,
post-pass) and Apex Form's +vital — could not tell a graze from a hit and fired on both. Now:
- **`edhaApplyIsGraze(options)`** — `options.edhaGraze` if an engine caller says so, else the
  breadcrumb the new `onClickApplyButton` wrap (`edhaWrapApplyClick`, at ready, libWrapper MIXED
  or prototype patch) stamps for the lifetime of a card's Apply click. `edhaWrapApplyDamage` reads
  it SYNCHRONOUSLY at the top (the post-pass runs after awaits, when the click is over) and hands
  `graze` to both Life readers.
- **The dial lives on the RULE (iron rule 2b):** `edha-mutation` grew `keenOnGraze` /
  `venomOnGraze`, `edha-regen-grant` grew `vitalOnGraze` (BooleanFields, initial true). The chooser
  carries each into the card (`data-edha-ongraze`) and the click bakes `mutation.onGraze`; the
  regen-grant use bakes `apexForm.vitalOnGraze`. Readers stand down on a graze ONLY for an explicit
  `false` — a flag baked before this deploy has no field and behaves exactly as before.
- **Audit (all melee mutation riders):** Bone Spurs "melee attacks DEAL additional Keen" → on;
  Venom Glands "melee HITS inflict Afflicted" → **off** (the one behaviour change); Apex Form
  "DEALS additional Vital on all attacks" → on. `edha-damage-rider` bonuses (Spearing Beak,
  Prognosis, Momentum's Edge, Kindle…) need no dial: they are roll-formula terms and the graze-clone
  guard already keeps them out of the graze roll, so they were hit-only all along.
- Proven: `tests/rider-graze-dial.test.js` (9 cases, each shown failing under a one-line
  reversion — gate dropped, `=== false` loosened, breadcrumb unstamped, dial dropped from the
  card); scratch `--ci` pack build green; BEFORE/AFTER compiled-pack diff = exactly 2 documents /
  2 rules changed (Adaptive Mutation `MutatePick000000`, Apex Form `ApexGrant0000000`).
- 🤖 checklist 2bW-18 (Venom on a graze: nothing) and 2bW-19 (Bone Spurs on a graze: applies).
## 2026-09-06 — Item 55: ONE senses rule for PCs and adversaries, R-56 (a) (**ENGINE + BUILD/DATA → pack REBUILD + a world bulk "⟳ Sync Adversaries from Pack", Ben's deploy; PR #240**)

R-56 (a), Ben: adversary SHEETS and TOKEN SIGHT use the Edha AWA table (0→10, 1→15, 2–3→20, 4→25,
5+→30), exactly as PCs do. Bench run 22 had measured three surfaces disagreeing about the SAME
creature: every world adversary's sheet read the cosmere ladder's **5** at AWA 0
(`edhaDeriveSheetStats` was character-only), every pack token carried a **flat 10** the build
hard-coded, and the sync pushed that 10 onto placed tokens whose actor still said 5.

- **Engine (F5 half):** the `type !== "character"` guard is gone at the three sites — senses in
  `edhaDeriveSheetStats` (now first, for every actor type; HP/Speed stay PC-only behind a later
  guard), the `preCreateActor` token-default hook (a blank-created adversary gets cosmere "sense"
  sight at table(AWA), but NOT the PC's HOVER(30) displayName — its name must not leak on hover),
  and the AWA `updateActor` watcher (prototype + placed tokens, single GM applier). The `ready`
  refresh sweep now resets EVERY actor, because a world adversary is prepared before the wrapper
  installs and would otherwise show 5 until its next update.
- **Build (REBUILD half):** `advSensesRangeFt(adv)` in `scripts/foundry-build-parts.js` — the
  block's explicit `senses` wins, else the table at AWA 0 (adversary blocks carry no attributes) —
  replaces the flat 10 in `advPrototypeToken`. `sensesRangeFtFromAwa` there is pinned equal to the
  engine's `edhaSensesRangeFtFromAwa` for AWA 0..7 so the two copies cannot drift.
- **Data:** `data/adversaries.json` — **Briar-Gone Grove gets `senses: 30`**, the one authored
  override (a rooted grove-heart has no eyes and perceives through its own soil; its reach is the
  arena, not an AWA-0 stare). The README's `senses` line now documents it as THE bespoke override.
- **Proven:** seven one-line reversions (each guard, the flat 10, a drifted table, the Grove's
  field, the sweep) each fail a named pin; scratch read-back before→after of the adversary pack:
  52 actors, **1 changed** (the Grove: token 10→30 + sheet override 30), 51 unchanged at 10 —
  the pack's number was already 10, so the rebuild moves the Grove and the ENGINE moves every world
  sheet (5→10). `node scripts/gates.js --ci` green (packs build + both validators).
- **Ben's deploy:** rebuild + deploy, then press **⟳ Sync Adversaries from Pack** (the world bulk
  sync this ruling authorises) so placed tokens re-stamp from the pack. 🤖 rows: the "Adversary
  tokens see like PCs" re-measure (AWA 0 → 10 on sheet AND token, whole population) and the new
  Briar-Gone Grove override row (sheet 30, token 30).

## 2026-09-06 — Item 63: Rallying Shout's reminder prints only for a DOWNED ally — `whenTarget` on `edha-note`, R-25 (c) (**ENGINE + AUTHORED → REBUILD heroic + ⟳ Sync Talents** — Ben's deploy; PR #239)

Fix pass 7a (item 47) stopped R-25 because the reminder is an **authored** `edha-note` rule
(`RouseRallying000` on Rousing Presence, `whenOwnsTalent: "Rallying Shout"`) and `edha-note` had no
target-condition field — the only engine-only route was a name-keyed branch, which rule 2b forbids.
This item ships the rebuild-class shape the ANSWERED block asked for:

- **Engine (F5 alone is inert here):** `edha-note` gains ONE generic schema field, **`whenTarget`**
  (choices blank | `downed`), read by the new pure gate **`edhaNoteTargetGate(whenTarget, target)`**
  next to `edhaRuleOwnsGate`. Blank = today's unconditional behaviour, so every other `edha-note` rule
  is untouched; `downed` = the note's subject creature (R-64 victim chain: `options.victim` →
  `options.target` → the clicking user's target) is at **0 health OR carries `unconscious`** — the two
  cases the card names. No target with the dial set = no card; an unknown mode fails OPEN (a typo
  never silences a note). The executor checks it right after the owns gate. No talent name enters the
  engine — the allowlist is unchanged (empty).
- **Authored (the consumer):** `data/authored/heroic-envoy.json` → `RouseRallying000.handler.whenTarget
  = "downed"`; its description no longer claims always-print. **Heroic pack REBUILD + ⟳ Sync Talents**
  before the bench can see it.
- **Proven:** `tests/note-target-gate.test.js` — ally at 32 HP → no card; at 0 (and below) → card;
  Unconscious above 0 → card; a rule without the field → card as before; each case shown failing under
  its own one-line reversion (M1–M5 in the PR body). Scratch `gates.js --ci` green with `EDHA_DATA`
  pinned to the branch's data; heroic pack parity against a main-data build: **204 documents, exactly 1
  differs** (Rousing Presence — that rule's `whenTarget` + description, nothing else).
- **🤖 bench:** checklist **2bM-6b** — three drives on Bench — Heroic after the rebuild (ally at full HP:
  no 📣 line; ally at 0 HP: line; ally Unconscious above 0: line). Rulings: R-25 marked SHIPPED

## 2026-09-06 — Item 64: the build's `edha-aoe-template` generator is retired, and the pack writers now refuse any `edha-*` handler type the engine does not register (**TOOLING-only** — the packs do not change, proven by content hash)

Fix-pass 7b (item 48, R-78) retired the `edha-aoe-template` handler, and its delta reported that
`scripts/foundry-build.js`'s `aoeRule()` still minted that type for any `TALENT_TARGETING` entry
with `.area` and no `.burst`. **Every `.area` entry checked:** Flame Surge, Set Charge, Mending
Aura, Thorn Field all carry `.burst` (they already emit `edha-burst`); **Lay Foundation** is the
only `.area`-alone entry, and it should NOT get an `edha-burst` — its mechanic is the persistent
Foundation zone, which its authored overlay already supplies as an `edha-zone` rule (the overlay
replacing the generated events is exactly why zero such rules ever reached a pack). So the
generator is gone, not routed: `grep -c aoeRule scripts/foundry-build.js` = 0; the `.area`-alone
case now emits nothing; `data/talent-targeting.json` is untouched.

**The guard** lives at the pack WRITERS, not in lint-refs pass 9: pass 9 holds AUTHORED rules in
`data/` to the engine's `registerItemEventHandlerType` calls, but a GENERATED rule never appears
in `data/`, which is why it could not see this one. `scripts/lib/handler-type-guard.js`
(`checkHandlerTypes`, pure) + `assertRegisteredHandlerTypes()` in both `writePack` and
`writeActorPack` — every document that reaches a pack passes through one of the two, whatever
generator or overlay produced it, and the build throws BEFORE the pack exists, naming the
document, rule id, and type. Registered set = the same `parseHandlerSchemas` parse pass 9 uses.

**Proven:** (a) parity — scratch builds before/after with `EDHA_DATA` pinned, content-hashed via
`readPack` + `stableStringify` with `_stats.createdTime/modifiedTime` stripped (item 58's method):
all five packs identical (`edha-leyline d69083a3…`, `edha-deity bb175843…`, `edha-heroic
b5675fc3…`, `edha-items f302d215…`, `edha-adversaries 2f511f17…`); the build report's `events`
count moves 37 → 36, which is Lay Foundation's masked rule no longer being generated. (b) mutation
— re-adding a generator for `edha-aoe-template` in `talentEvents` makes the build exit 1 with
`"Unity of Purpose" rule … has handler type "edha-aoe-template", which the engine never registers`;
restored → PASS. `tests/handler-type-guard.test.js` pins the guard on fixtures, against the real
engine (retired type rejected, `edha-burst` accepted), and that the generator stays gone. Nothing
🤖 — no table behaviour changed.

## 2026-09-06 — Item 43: the phone board opens on "Needs you" — open-ruling and Ben-only cards, a stale-heartbeat banner (**DOCS/TOOLING** — no engine, no data)

Ben, phone chat: scrolling the whole dashboard to find one answerable ruling was why rulings sat
unanswered. `docs/pm-board-mobile.html` now opens on a **Needs you** view: one status line (PM
awake/stopped, what is running, blocked-on-you yes/no), a stale-heartbeat banner (PM says awake but
`pm/state.generatedAt` is over 60 minutes old — the 09-06 stall), a card per OPEN ruling, a card per
`benOnly` ask, and a ⚑ count-and-link. Everything else (Now detail, Snapshot, Budget, Queue, the old
For Ben list, the Inbox composer, Run log, the full Dashboard) moved under a collapsed `#more`
`<details>`, state in `localStorage`, every access try/catch'd.

- **`scripts/build-dashboard.js`**: new `parseOpenRulings(md)` walks `parseRulings()`'s own
  sections/blocks (no new markdown parser) — a ruling is open when its own text carries none of
  `**ANSWERED` / `**VETOED` / `**SETTLED` (the last catches the one §B stub that restates a §K
  ruling without repeating its resolution, R-4); §J/§K are skipped outright; §I entries carry
  `applied: true`. `mobileSnapshot()` fills each entry's `blocks` count via new `countCitations()`
  (checklist + repo tabs; a migration code like `2bR-18` never counts as a citation of ruling
  `R-18`) and ships `openRulings: [{id, section, ask, default, applied, blocks}]` in the dash
  **index**, not a chunk — the cards render with no chunk fetch.
- **`scripts/pm-state.js`**: new `parseBenOnly(md)` reads the board's "Waiting on Ben" line into
  `state.benOnly` — one bullet per ask once the PM writes it that way, else a paren-depth-aware `;`
  split on today's inline numbered prose (a semicolon inside an aside, e.g. "(seven items
  bench-pending)", never counts — only a `(<digit>)` marker does).
- **Card controls write the same inbox note the composer would**, via a new shared `sendNote()`:
  **[Go with the default]** → `Re Rulings › <section> › R-n. <question>: <default>`; **[Other…]** →
  an inline textarea, same prefix; §I "applied — veto?" cards get **[Keep]** / **[Veto…]**;
  Ben-only cards get **[Done]** → `Re Waiting on Ben › <ask>: done.`. No control scrolls to the
  composer.
- **Proven:** `tests/pm-state.test.js` pins R-18/R-48 open and R-41/R-42/R-54 ANSWERED-closed
  against the real `EDHA_RULINGS.md`, plus a synthetic fixture for the §B-stub / §I-applied /
  §K-skip shape and the citation counter's migration-code guard. Verified in a local static
  preview (`pm-state.js --inject`): both real open rulings render with correct default/no-default
  text, the full-text expander pulls the real ruling body off the already-loaded Rulings tab, `#more`
  toggles and persists, and the page still renders with `dbRef` absent (buttons toast instead of
  throwing). The tracked page keeps `{}` in both snapshot slots; `EDHA_DASHBOARD.html` is byte-for-
  byte unchanged (`openRulings` is mobile-only) and its `--check` gate still passes.
- **Found, not R-80:** the item's own brief names "today R-18, R-48, R-80" as the open set; R-80
  does not exist anywhere in the repo (`EDHA_RULINGS.md`, `docs/PM_BOARD.md`, a full-repo grep) —
